Axon's own GetNetworkHealth/NetworkHealthSummary is a separate realm-aggregate contract, not what this ability should emit.
- **现状：** shipped in CLI — handler src/runtime/agents/network_health_ability.rs:54-126, manifest abilities/system/observe.network_health.ability.toml, composition helper src/runtime/agents/federation_probe.rs (federation.resolve + per-peer observe.health). Frame tag F-03 surface: leave the cross-language contracts (federation.resolve, observe.health) in Axon and do NOT sink this local-vantage link/probe shape into Axon (it would over-sink a device runtime view into the neutral protocol). Optional CLI follow-up: assert the per-link status enum (reachable/unreachable/unjoined/probe_failed) stays a documented CLI-local vocabulary so it is never mistaken for a wire contract. No RFC-003 sec0 note required (no Axon-repo work).

### 179. ✅ Invocation ledger (redb-backed audit log)

- **裁决：** `CORRECT. The record shape, redb table definitions, JSON value codec, X25519+ChaCha20 payload sealing, and trace-DAG projection are all in easynet_axon::invocation::ledger (sdk/rust/src/invocation/ledger.rs) — the file header names it "the durable invocation-history record shape used by CLI, daemon, and future Axon runtime surfaces" and "SDK protocol object shared by application hosts." Two different-language nodes (or an external auditor) must AGREE on this record/seal format to verify history, so the SHAPE correctly sits in Axon. The CLI only opens the file at a config-derived path, threads the Arc into the kernel, and exposes a read ability — pure RUNTIME/EXECUTOR/FRONT-DOOR wiring. No protocol fork: CLI defines no record/codec/seal of its own; it imports them.`
- **当前所在：** cli+axon
- **符合 EasyNet 价值：** YES — the endgame requires "auditable by easynet-verify" and "user owns identity+state+audit". A per-device, owner-held, sealed invocation history (full caller/callee/subject/ability URAs, receipt-chain anchors, DAG trace links) is exactly the audit substrate that easynet-verify reads. It lives on the owner device and is keyed by URA.
- **CLI 预期实现：** Owns the device-local wiring: open easynet_axon::invocation::InvocationLedger at ledger_dir/invocations.redb (open_invocation_ledger, easynet-daemon.rs:422-441), graceful degrade to None when config/file unavailable, thread the Arc into Kernel/registry so Invoke writes (lines 195,221,257), and surface read access via the invocation_history_ability front door. CLI must NOT redefine the record/codec/seal — it imports them.
- **Axon 预期实现：** Owns the neutral contract: InvocationLedger record struct, redb table layout, JSON codec, LedgerEventPayload Digest/Sealed semantics, full-URA preservation, receipt-chain anchors, and trace-DAG projection (sdk/rust/src/invocation/ledger.rs). This is what any-language host or auditor must agree on. Already provided.
- **现状：** shipped, correctly split. Contract: /Users/macbook.silan.tech/Documents/GitHub/EasyNet-Axon/sdk/rust/src/invocation/ledger.rs (records, RECORDS/REQUEST_INDEX/TRACE_INDEX tables, sealing, query/fetch keys). CLI wiring: /Users/macbook.silan.tech/Documents/GitHub/EasyNet-Cli/src/bin/easynet-daemon.rs:195,221,257,422-441 and read surface /Users/macbook.silan.tech/Documents/GitHub/EasyNet-Cli/src/runtime/agents/invocation_history_ability.rs. The "cli+axon" today is the correct division, not a fork.
- **行动：** No relayer needed — placement is correct. F-05 cleanup only: (1) the CLI read ability recomputes ledger_path_from_config() per call instead of reusing the already-opened Arc<InvocationLedger> threaded at boot — collapse to the shared handle to remove the second path-resolution code path and the divergence risk if ledger_dir changes; (2) confirm easynet-verify consumes this Axon record shape (not a CLI projection). Both are CLI-repo work; no RFC-003 sec0 Axon-repo note required since the Axon contract is unchanged.

## 经济 / Economics（6）

### 182. ⬜ Ability pricing / tariff declaration

- **裁决：** `MISSING — and, per the boundary criterion, the missing piece belongs in Axon, not CLI. A price/tariff is the textbook "two different-language nodes must AGREE on it to interoperate" case: a consumer node in any language must read the same machine-decidable rate to decide-before-invoke, so it is CONTRACT shape on CapabilityDescriptor/AbilityDescriptor. Today Axon's CapabilityDescriptor (types.proto:746) has tags/requirements/signature but NO price field; the only economic surface is reserved-inert (ObjectiveWeights.cost is a 0-1 optimization weight not a rate; EASYNET_HOOK_ECONOMY_SETTLE; CostEstimate is a named-only future attachment point in docs/design/planner-interface-v1.md with no type). The CLI CostMeta/CostKind (ability_spec.rs:211-259) is NOT a fork of this and NOT a substitute: it is a coarse qualitative bucket (free/external_metered/llm_metered/unknown) plus a free-form human label, and its own doc-comment (lines 207-209) disclaims monetary accounting. So there is no protocol fork to undo — there is a genuine gap in the contract layer.`
- **当前所在：** none-missing
- **符合 EasyNet 价值：** PARTIAL — the endgame ("ability lives on owner device, addressed by URA, governed by Axon, decentralized economic flourishing" per Axon README §60) explicitly bets on abilities being economically tradeable, so a price/tariff a consumer can read pre-invoke directly serves owner-monetization. But it is NOT a load-bearing primitive for the identity+state+audit+URA+Surface core; settlement is reserved (EASYNET_HOOK_ECONOMY_SETTLE, "credit economy") and inert today, so value is forward-looking, not current.
- **CLI 预期实现：** CLI surfaces, does not define, the tariff. RUNTIME/EXECUTOR: read the Axon-defined price field from the manifest and (a) annotate it onto the MCP/discover surface next to the existing cost_kind line so a human/LLM front-door consumer sees the rate, (b) record realized charge into the invocation ledger (device.invocation.history already advertises "for audit and billing", history.list.ability.toml:3). CLI must NOT invent the price field shape itself — a CLI-local price schema would be a protocol fork (the same trap CostMeta narrowly avoids by being explicitly non-monetary qualitative metadata).
- **Axon 预期实现：** Axon owns the shape: add an optional machine-decidable tariff/price field to the ability contract (CapabilityDescriptor / AbilityDescriptor and the manifest spec) — at minimum a structured {unit, amount, currency-or-credit, per-call|per-token|metered} so any-language consumer can decide-before-invoke without string-parsing a human label. Define how/whether the agreed price binds into the Receipt-adjacent ledger for audit (without polluting the Receipt itself per DEC-F01-Q9). This is the neutral cross-language economic contract; the inert ObjectiveWeights.cost / ECONOMY_SETTLE reservations are the natural anchor to formalize.
- **现状：** missing (as a price/tariff). No price/tariff field in EasyNet-Cli src/core/ability_spec.rs, none in abilities/system/*.toml, and none in EasyNet-Axon core/proto/axon/v1/*.proto (CapabilityDescriptor types.proto:746 has no price). Partial-adjacent only: CLI CostMeta/CostKind qualitative bucket (ability_spec.rs:211-259) and Axon reserved-inert economic context (types.proto:985-1015 ObjectiveWeights.cost + EASYNET_HOOK_ECONOMY_SETTLE; planner-interface-v1.md names CostEstimate with no concrete type).
- **行动：** F-07 ontology-absorption — Axon-repo work (RFC-003 sec0 note: this is a contract-shape change to CapabilityDescriptor/AbilityDescriptor + manifest spec, so it must land as an Axon RFC/proto change first, sec0-scoped, not a CLI-local field). Next step: open an Axon ontology proposal to define an optional structured tariff field on the ability descriptor (anchor it to the existing reserved economic context so it stays neutral and ignorable until EasyNet activates), then CLI follows by surfacing the Axon-defined field on the MCP/discover front door and ledger. Do NOT add a price field to CLI ability_spec.rs first — that would fork the economic contract into one runtime.

### 183. ⬜ Agreement / settlement primitive

- **裁决：** `MISSING — and the missing home is Axon. Two different-language nodes (caller vs ability owner) must AGREE on offer/accept terms and the settlement record to interoperate; that mutual agreement is by the boundary criterion a CONTRACT, so it belongs in Axon's neutral proto/SDK alongside the existing Invocation Ledger. It is NOT forked (no CLI-local copy exists), NOT over-sunk, NOT product-UI leak. Axon today has only a stub: EASYNET_HOOK_ECONOMY_SETTLE=3 and EasyNetContext.budget_limit/reputation_min_score marked "future" (core/proto/axon/v1/types.proto:1008, 1019-1024) — placeholders, no Agreement/Settlement/Offer/Invoice message.`
- **当前所在：** none-missing
- **符合 EasyNet 价值：** PARTIAL — it serves the endgame's "governed + auditable, strangers settle" half (README L38/L40 names settle as a core promise; the receipt becomes anchored to an economic obligation, not just forensic truth). But it is not on the critical path to the minimum endgame (user owns identity+state+audit; ability on owner device, addressed by URA, returns Surface, governed by Axon). Audit/account already stands without it; economic value sits ON TOP of the ledger. So real, but a later ring, not load-bearing for the first vertical.
- **CLI 预期实现：** CLI is the EXECUTOR/FRONT-DOOR for the primitive once Axon defines it: `agreement propose|accept|reject|counter|list|show` verbs (human front door) + MCP tool exposure (agent front door), driving Axon RPCs and recording the resulting settlement reference into the local invocation record. CLI must NOT define the agreement shape itself — doing so would be a protocol fork. Today: n/a / blocked — CLI review (docs/cli-command-review-2026-05-30.md §7, lines 630-637) correctly defers the whole `wallet`/`agreement` axis as cross-repo, protocol-blocked.
- **Axon 预期实现：** Axon SHOULD define the cross-language settlement contract: an Agreement message (parties = caller URA + owner URA, terms/SLA/price), an offer→accept→settle lifecycle (Propose/Accept/Reject/Counter/Settle), and a Settlement record that references/anchors a ledger event (LedgerEventPayload at types.proto:490) so the receipt binds to an economic obligation. Must be neutral, sealed-payload compatible with the existing ledger visibility model, and importable in any language. The EASYNET_HOOK_ECONOMY_SETTLE hook is the integration seam already reserved for it.
- **现状：** missing — no Agreement/Settlement/Offer/Accept/Invoice/Escrow/Wallet message in any repo (verified by grep across EasyNet-Cli, EasyNet-Axon, EasyNet). Only present as: (1) narrative promise — EasyNet-Axon/README.md L38,L40; (2) a reserved-but-empty hook — core/proto/axon/v1/types.proto:1008 (EASYNET_HOOK_ECONOMY_SETTLE=3) + future fields L1019-1024; (3) explicitly roadmapped/blocked in CLI docs — EasyNet-Cli/docs/cli-command-review-2026-05-30.md §7 (lines 95, 623-637, 849, 878) and docs/cli-axon-interface-review-2026-05-30.md (lines 124-128, 191, 201). The adjacent Invocation Ledger (audit) IS shipped (types.proto:465-516), confirming the deliberate Account-vs-Economic split.
- **行动：** F-07 ontology-absorption (Axon-repo work). Next step: open an AXON RFC defining the Agreement/Settlement contract — message shapes (parties as URAs, terms, lifecycle Propose/Accept/Reject/Counter/Settle) anchored to the existing LedgerEventPayload, wired to the reserved EASYNET_HOOK_ECONOMY_SETTLE seam. RFC-003 sec0 note: this is a §0-classification "new protocol primitive" — must be filed and ratified at the Axon spec layer (per RFC §0 classification scheme, AXON-RFC-001-restatement-mapping.md §0) BEFORE any CLI `agreement`/`wallet` verb is specced; CLI side stays gated until the Axon target version exists (per cli-axon-interface-review §M2). Do not let CLI grow a local agreement shape in the interim — that would be a protocol fork.

### 184. ⬜ Per-invocation cost/usage metering

- **裁决：** `MISSING — and the gap straddles the boundary cleanly so it must NOT be solved CLI-local. Apply the criterion: a cost/usage figure that a consumer must trust and re-verify against the callee's signed receipt is something two different-language nodes MUST AGREE on (payer and payee, possibly across languages/hubs). Therefore the SHAPE belongs in Axon: a usage/cost field set on InvocationLedgerRecord and on the signed InvocationReceipt (so it is inside the hashed, callee-signed canonical bytes — otherwise the number is unverifiable and billing is repudiable). Verified absent: easynet_axon::invocation::ledger.rs InvocationLedgerRecord has elapsed_ms but 0 occurrences of cost/token/amount/price/meter; invoke.proto InvocationReceipt (line 659) has index/state/hashes/signatures/bindings but no cost field. The instinct to "just add it to ledger_dir" would be a protocol fork (CLI-local cost the consumer cannot verify). Watch: catalog-level CostMeta/CostKind already exists in CLI (ability_spec.rs:198-270) but is explicitly a static discovery label ("runtime usage accounting per-call is out of scope") — that is correctly CLI and is NOT this feature.`
- **当前所在：** none-missing
- **符合 EasyNet 价值：** YES — the endgame ("owner monetizes abilities on their device, consumer pays per Invoke, auditable by easynet-verify") is literally an economic loop. A receipt that an owner can bill against, and a consumer can independently verify the charge for, is the missing rail that turns "governable invocation" into "monetizable invocation." Without a cost figure bound into the auditable receipt chain, the URA-addressed ability is reachable and decidable but not chargeable.
- **CLI 预期实现：** CLI is the EXECUTOR that measures the real numbers and the RUNTIME that writes the ledger, so it owns capture and population — not the contract shape. Concretely: (1) the executor edge already harvests real usage (drivers/claude_code.rs:76-81 captures input_tokens/output_tokens/cache_*/total_cost_usd for the stream UI) — thread that into build_unary_ledger_record which today only computes elapsed_ms; (2) populate the new Axon usage/cost field on the InvocationLedgerRecord it persists to ledger_dir/invocations.redb and on the admission receipt it signs; (3) expose it on the `invocation` CLI verb / MCP surface for the human/agent front door. CLI does NOT define the field shape.
- **Axon 预期实现：** Axon defines the neutral, cross-language SHAPE: a structured Usage/Cost message (e.g. usage_units map: tokens_in/tokens_out/compute_ms, plus an optional priced amount with currency + price_basis) added to (a) invoke.proto InvocationReceipt so it lands inside the canonical hashed + callee-signed bytes (RFC-001 §4.3) — making the charge non-repudiable and re-verifiable offline by easynet-verify — and (b) the SDK InvocationLedgerRecord / builder so every language binding emits and reads it identically. Must be optional/absent-by-default so existing receipts and the §5.2 two-RPC surface stay valid (DEC-F01-Q9: do not pollute the realtime path; cost rides the receipt, not a live stream).
- **现状：** missing (the contract field) + partial (the raw data). Contract: absent in Axon — invoke.proto InvocationReceipt (EasyNet-Axon/core/proto/axon/v1/invoke.proto:659) and SDK InvocationLedgerRecord (EasyNet-Axon/sdk/rust/src/invocation/ledger.rs, cost-term grep = 0) carry only elapsed_ms. CLI ledger (EasyNet-Cli/.../daemon_invocation_service.rs build_unary_ledger_record, written to ledger_dir/invocations.redb per easynet-daemon.rs) is a pass-through of that Axon record, so it also has no cost. Raw numbers DO exist but unbound: CLI drivers/claude_code.rs:76-81 already computes total_cost_usd + token counts for the stream UI only. Catalog-level CostMeta (CLI ability_spec.rs:198-270) exists but is a static label, not per-invocation metering.
- **行动：** new (F-07 ontology-absorption shaped): Open an Axon RFC-003 sec0 ticket to extend the invocation ontology with a verifiable Usage/Cost shape on InvocationReceipt (inside canonical signed bytes) + InvocationLedgerRecord/builder, optional and back-compatible. Per RFC-003 sec0, this is an Axon-repo protocol change and must land Axon-first as the neutral contract before CLI consumes it — do NOT add a cost field to ledger_dir locally (that is a fork). Then CLI follow-up: thread the already-captured executor usage (claude_code.rs:76-81) through build_unary_ledger_record into the new Axon field and surface it on the invocation verb/MCP. Note this is "Cli runs ahead, Axon absorbs back" (F-01 sec7): CLI already has the raw signal; Axon must absorb the shape so it becomes cross-language verifiable.

### 185. 🔴 Usage quota / rate accounting per consumer

- **裁决：** `FORKED-RISK / split-correct: the CONTRACT SHAPE already lives correctly in Axon (RateLimitInfo{quota_remaining, quota_limit, reset_at_unix_ms, retry_after_ms} in core/proto/axon/v1/types.proto:223, wired into invoke responses at invoke.proto:129/302/593/617 as "caller's quota status"). Two different-language nodes already agree on how quota status crosses the wire — correct, do NOT re-define it in CLI. The MISSING piece (the per-caller counter that decrements per invoke and resets on the window) is serving-node ENFORCEMENT/state, which belongs in the CLI runtime, not Axon. WRONG move to watch: re-declaring a quota struct CLI-local (protocol fork of RateLimitInfo) or sinking the counting state machine into the neutral Axon protocol (over-sink). So: contract CORRECT in Axon; enforcement MISSING in CLI.`
- **当前所在：** none-missing
- **符合 EasyNet 价值：** PARTIAL — quota caps directly serve "ability lives on owner device, governed and auditable": an owner capping a consumer's invocation usage is an economic governance act on their own device. But it is a secondary governance refinement (the permission gate already decides allow/deny); quota only meters an already-permitted caller, so it is value-adding, not load-bearing for the identity/URA/Surface endgame.
- **CLI 预期实现：** CLI daemon (RUNTIME/EXECUTOR) maintains a per-(consumer-URA, ability) usage counter in a service store alongside the existing governance stores (src/services/ — peer to nonce_replay_store.rs / hub_published_ability_store.rs): decrement on each admitted invoke, track window reset, reject with retry_after when exhausted, and populate the existing Axon RateLimitInfo on the invoke response. Owner-facing CLI verb to set/inspect a consumer's cap. Today src/services has no quota/usage store and the daemon does not read/populate RateLimitInfo (grep confirms zero references in src/).
- **Axon 预期实现：** Already provided — RateLimitInfo wire shape (types.proto:223) carried on Invoke/Async/Stream responses. No new Axon contract needed; the neutral shape for "quota status crossing the wire" exists. Axon must NOT grow the enforcement counter or reset-window state machine (that is per-device runtime state, not cross-language contract). At most a future minor: confirm RateLimitInfo field semantics are sufficient (they are: remaining/limit/reset_at/retry_after).
- **现状：** missing (enforcement) — Axon ships only the wire type: RateLimitInfo at /Users/macbook.silan.tech/Documents/GitHub/EasyNet-Axon/core/proto/axon/v1/types.proto:223 and field wiring in invoke.proto (129/302/593/617), with NO counting/enforcement logic in runtime-rs (only generated .pb structs found). CLI has zero quota tracking: no match in /Users/macbook.silan.tech/Documents/GitHub/EasyNet-Cli/src (the only "quota/rate" hits are unrelated comments in eal/ast.rs:139, runtime/workspace.rs:787, agents/profiles/mcp.rs:848, cli/start.rs:861). Permission gate (governance) exists but no usage counter.
- **行动：** new + F-05: build the per-caller quota counter in the CLI daemon as a new src/services usage store (decrement-on-admit, window reset, reject-with-retry_after), and populate the EXISTING Axon RateLimitInfo on invoke responses — do not define a CLI-local quota type. No Axon proto change required (RFC-003 sec0 n/a; contract shape already landed); if a future RFC formalizes enforcement semantics, scope it to documenting RateLimitInfo population rules, not adding enforcement state to Axon.

### 186. ⬜ Wallet / balance / credit

- **裁决：** `MISSING (with a thin reserved seam already correctly placed). Apply the criterion in three parts: (a) The wallet/ledger STORE-and-LOGIC is NOT a cross-language wire contract two nodes must agree on byte-for-byte — it is a stateful service. Per INV-3 (ability is a method, no global registry) it belongs as an EasyNet ability (EasyNet-Ledger, designed in EasyNet-Ecosystem-Plan.md:232-264 as official-but-replaceable), NOT baked into the neutral protocol. Sinking the ledger engine into Axon would be product/runtime over-sink. (b) The ONLY pieces that must agree cross-language are the seam fields — and those already sit in Axon correctly as reserved passthrough stubs: EasyNetContext.budget_limit and EASYNET_HOOK_ECONOMY_SETTLE (types.proto:1004-1018), explicitly "MUST be ignored" until activation. (c) Nothing leaks: Receipt carries no cost/credit field (kept clean per DEC-F01-Q9; the only "cost" at types.proto:999 is a routing objective weight, not a charge). So the feature is genuinely MISSING as an implemented capability; not forked, not over-sunk, not leaked.`
- **当前所在：** none-missing
- **符合 EasyNet 价值：** YES — the endgame "user owns identity+state+audit" extends to owning an economic account: to pay for invoking another owner's ability or be paid for serving one's own, the owner needs a wallet/balance addressable by their identity URA and settled against auditable receipts. Without it, ability exchange across owners has no value transfer, only free calls.
- **CLI 预期实现：** Runtime/executor seam only, no economic logic of its own. When the daemon executes an invocation it should (1) attach the caller-owner's EasyNetContext.budget_limit on the outgoing invoke if a budget is set, and (2) on completion fire the EASYNET_HOOK_ECONOMY_SETTLE hook by invoking the owner's chosen ledger ability (e.g. ledger.settle(invocation_id)) as just another ability call through the executor. CLI must NOT hold balances, do double-entry accounting, or expose a "wallet" product surface — that is EasyNet-Ledger's job. CLI front-door may expose a thin verb (easynet wallet balance) that simply invokes the bound ledger ability, the same way it dispatches any other ability.
- **Axon 预期实现：** Contract seam only — already partially present and correctly scoped. Axon should keep/finalize: budget_limit and reputation_min_score in EasyNetContext, and the EASYNET_HOOK_ECONOMY_SETTLE hook point (types.proto:1004-1018), as neutral, language-agnostic fields that any SDK can carry. Axon must NOT define the ledger account model, balance arithmetic, escrow, or transfer RPCs — those are a service/ability contract, not the URA/invoke/receipt protocol. Receipt must stay free of cost/credit fields (DEC-F01-Q9); settlement reads the receipt's invocation_id by reference rather than embedding money in the receipt. Optionally Axon may later ratify a small canonical "settlement reference" shape (invocation_id -> ledger ability URA) so settlement is cross-language interoperable, but the wallet itself stays an EasyNet ability.
- **现状：** missing — only reserved/passthrough scaffolding and a design doc exist. Axon proto has dormant stubs: EasyNet-Axon/core/proto/axon/v1/types.proto:991 (credit economy comment), :1008 (EASYNET_HOOK_ECONOMY_SETTLE), :1015 (budget_limit), all explicitly "MUST be ignored" until EasyNet activation; no implementation in EasyNet-Axon/core/runtime-rs/src (zero hits for economy_settle/ledger.settle). CLI has no economics code (grep hits are load-balancing, stream flow-control credits, and UI files — false positives). EasyNet has only a design: EasyNet/document/EasyNet-Ecosystem-Plan.md:232-264 specs EasyNet-Ledger as a Go+PostgreSQL Axon-Node ability (ledger.balance/transfer/escrow/settle), unbuilt.
- **行动：** new — do NOT touch CLI or Axon runtime yet. Step 1: open a new frame to spec EasyNet-Ledger as the canonical economics ability per EasyNet-Ecosystem-Plan.md:232-264 (account keyed by identity URA, double-entry, ledger.settle bound to EASYNET_HOOK_ECONOMY_SETTLE). Step 2 (Axon-repo, RFC-003 sec0 note: any change to the proto seam is a protocol-surface edit and must land via an Axon RFC, not an ad-hoc proto edit — coordinate the seam ratification before code): confirm/ratify that the existing EasyNetContext.budget_limit + EASYNET_HOOK_ECONOMY_SETTLE seam is the agreed activation contract and that Receipt stays cost-free. Step 3 (F-05 cleanup / CLI): once the ability exists, wire the CLI executor's post-invocation hook to call the bound ledger ability — runtime seam only, no balance logic in CLI. Tag: new (greenfield economics primitive), with the wallet engine landing in EasyNet, the activation seam in Axon, and the dispatch hook in CLI.

### 187. ✅ ledger_dir storage path

- **裁决：** `CORRECT (the device path). The ledger record shape, receipt-chain DAG, sealed-payload format, and even the "billing/invocations.redb" filename convention are the cross-language CONTRACT and already live in Axon (sdk/rust/src/invocation/ledger.rs). What CLI holds is only WHERE that ledger lands on this device — a runtime/persistence concern, which is correctly CLI per the boundary criterion (how this device runs). It is not a protocol fork: CLI does not redefine the ledger, it calls Axon's InvocationLedger::open(path). The CLI config field has been renamed to ledger_dir while accepting legacy billing_dir as a backward-compatible alias.`
- **当前所在：** cli
- **符合 EasyNet 价值：** PARTIAL — it serves the audit pillar (every terminal invocation persists to the ledger so the owner can run easynet-verify), which is core to the endgame. But the "economics/billing" intent the name promises is unrealized: no cost/price/amount/settlement value, so it serves only the audit-trail half of the value, not metering.
- **CLI 预期实现：** A configurable on-device storage path for the audit ledger (RUNTIME/persistence role) — resolve default vs configured dir, create it, and hand the path to Axon's InvocationLedger::open. This is exactly what daemon_config.rs does. CLI should NOT carry any cost/price/settlement logic. Recommended: rename to ledger_dir / audit_dir so the name matches the sole responsibility (holding invocations.redb).
- **Axon 预期实现：** The ledger SHAPE: record format, receipt-chain anchors, sealed event payloads, and the documented "billing/invocations.redb" filename convention — all already in Axon sdk/rust/src/invocation/ledger.rs (InvocationLedger, LedgerSink). If true metering/economics is ever wanted, the cost/amount/settlement FIELDS must be added to the Axon Receipt/ledger record (cross-language agreement), never invented CLI-local. Today axon_expected for the path resolution itself is n/a — Axon takes a path arg, it does not pick the dir.
- **现状：** shipped (audit-store only). default_ledger_dir(), DaemonConfig::ledger_dir(), and the serde alias for legacy billing_dir live in src/persistence/daemon_config.rs; consumed to build the redb path at src/bin/easynet-daemon.rs and src/services/invocation_transport/boot.rs. Backing contract shipped in Axon at sdk/rust/src/invocation/ledger.rs. Zero settlement/price/amount logic confirmed by grep across CLI persistence + daemon and Axon ledger.rs — the "billing" cost_label strings in mcp.rs/ability_spec.rs are unrelated UI hints, not metering.
- **行动：** Done for the CLI field/API name: ledger_dir is the active config/accessor name and billing_dir remains a compatibility alias. Keep "billing/invocations.redb" on-disk layout unless Axon migrates the documented convention in lockstep. Do NOT add cost/settlement logic here; if metering is ever scoped, that is a separate F-07 ontology item landing cost fields on the Axon Receipt/ledger contract first.
